- **codex**: Delegate a substantial coding, research, diagnosis, or review task to OpenAI Codex running in a sandbox. Use for a genuinely independent second implementation or investigation pass, for work large enough to run for many minutes on its own, or whenever the user asks for Codex. Codex model, reasoning effort, and sandbox policy can be requested in plain prose in the prompt. Do not use for small edits the main thread can finish quickly itself. ([PeterSR/claude-code-codex-subagent](https://github.com/PeterSR/claude-code-codex-subagent/blob/HEAD/agents/codex.md) · ★4)
- **codex**: Delegate a task to OpenAI Codex, which runs headlessly in this project, and get its answer back. Use for second opinions, independent code reviews, or self-contained subtasks you want another model family to do. Several can run in parallel or in the background. Mention a Codex model (e.g. gpt-6-sol), whether Codex may edit files, or a session_id to continue an earlier Codex run. Return the bridge job id and result unchanged; a running job or error is also a valid relay result. Not for T3 Code chats; there, use T3's own delegate_task instead. ([rennerdo30/agent-bridge](https://github.com/rennerdo30/agent-bridge/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude/agents/codex.md) · ★4)

- **codex**: OpenAI's operator-installed Codex CLI. The richer integration under `src/agents/codex/` drives Codex's native app-server: every shell command or file change it proposes gets an in-chat Approve/Deny card, and sessions resume by their rollout thread id (`codex-approvals` runtime id). - **codex-sol-operator**: Runs ONE named `mise run` task that mutates git or host state — `land`, `automerge`, `sync`, `verify-local` — and reports its real exit code. - **codex-sol-staleness-auditor**: Audits repo instruction/reference prose—rules, AGENTS.md/CLAUDE.md, docs, receipts, and memory—for stale claims. ([ray-manaloto/dotfiles-legacy](https://github.com/ray-manaloto/dotfiles-legacy/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/codex-sol-staleness-auditor.md) · ★5)

- **codex-sounding-board**: 技術的な壁打ち（brainstorming）の相手として OpenAI Codex CLI を使い、設計判断・アーキテクチャ選択・ライブラリ選定などのセカンドオピニオンを取る。Claude の提案をそのまま採用する前、二者択一で迷うとき、大きなリファクタリング方針を決めるとき、などに使う。 ([ignission/tally](https://github.com/ignission/tally/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/codex-sounding-board.md))
- **codex-spark-worker**: Implement scoped features, bug fixes, tests, and refactors with clear acceptance criteria. ([fpcMotif/nix-darwin](https://github.com/fpcMotif/nix-darwin/blob/HEAD/modules/home/agent-instructions/omp/agent/agents/codex-spark-worker.md))
- **codex-spawner**: Spawn and manage Codex CLI processes in isolated git worktrees ([wowoyong/agent-mux](https://github.com/wowoyong/agent-mux/blob/HEAD/agents/codex-spawner.md) · ★1)
- **codex-spec-guardian**: Validates proposed code changes against Codex Studio's frozen architecture. Reads project_brief.md, build_rules.md, prompt_instructions.md and runs `node verify-frozen.js`. Use before accepting ANY generated HTML, CSS, or JS — especially changes touching &lt;head&gt;, DOM structure, work-card data-id, theme-color tag, tag filters, token names, or script order. Returns PASS or FAIL with quoted rule violations. Read-only. ([Gorgutc/codex](https://github.com/Gorgutc/codex/blob/HEAD/.claude/skills/codex-studio-rules/references/claude-original/agents/codex-spec-guardian.md) · 2 copies)
- **codex-split-a-task-across-model-pinned-threads**: For a task with distinct stages, run separate Codex threads each pinned to a different model (a cheap cloud model for research, a local model to shortlist, the frontier model for final synthesis) and explicitly tell Codex to pass results from one thread to the next. ([model-bender/claudex-setup](https://github.com/model-bender/claudex-setup/blob/HEAD/lessons/agents/codex-split-a-task-across-model-pinned-threads.md))
- **codex-standard**: General-purpose Codex agent for code changes, debugging, refactors, reviews, planning docs. USAGE RESTRICTION — Codex budget is limited this month. Do NOT use proactively. Only use when the human explicitly requests Codex. Ask permission before delegating to this agent. ([j-greig/wibandwob-dos](https://github.com/j-greig/wibandwob-dos/blob/HEAD/.pi/agents/codex-standard.md) · ★7)
- **codex-statuszeile**: Festgehalten am 04.09.2026, 21:09 Uhr. CLI 0.153.2. ([Pepsi1978/proggs](https://github.com/Pepsi1978/proggs/blob/HEAD/bugs/agents/codex-statuszeile.md) · ★1)
- **codex-strategist**: Calls OpenAI Codex CLI to synthesize multiple analysis reports and provide big-picture strategic review. Cross-checks gap analysis, tradeoff analysis, verification planning, and external research for contradictions and blind spots. ([team-attention/hoyeon](https://github.com/team-attention/hoyeon/blob/HEAD/agents/codex-strategist.md) · ★173 · 2 copies)
- **codex-structured**: Read-only structured-output relay for blended ultracode-xmodel workflows. Runs one short Codex verifier, juror, or second-opinion task against a caller-supplied JSON Schema and returns schema-valid JSON verbatim. ([pablogcloud/ultracode-xmodel](https://github.com/pablogcloud/ultracode-xmodel/blob/HEAD/agents/codex-structured.md) · ★1)
- **codex-style-agent**: Review AI-assisted implementations for completeness and follow-through — whether the change was carried to working, verified code rather than stopped at a plan, a placeholder, or a partial fix. Use after an agent has implemented a feature and before opening a PR. Do not use it as a general code review (that is `/review`), a security review (`security-reviewer`), or the final pre-merge gate (`judge`). ([coreindustries/core-ai-template](https://github.com/coreindustries/core-ai-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/codex-style-agent.md) · ★1)
- **codex-style-agent**: A specialized code review agent that evaluates implementations against codex-max principles and this project's AI agent development standards. ([coreindustries/core-python-template](https://github.com/coreindustries/core-python-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/codex-style-agent.md))
- **codex-subagents-dont-auto-invoke**: Codex's docs state it won't spawn a subagent unless you explicitly ask, while Claude Code decides on its own to spin up planner/explorer/reviewer agents. A workflow ported to Codex that relied on auto-delegation quietly runs single-threaded in the main context. ([model-bender/claudex-setup](https://github.com/model-bender/claudex-setup/blob/HEAD/lessons/agents/codex-subagents-dont-auto-invoke.md))
- **codex-swarm-background**: Direct documentation assignment. Isolated checkout; explicit path staging and manual validation/push instead of a recurring sync timer. No experiment dispatch. ([dmarzzz/swarm-dynamics-lab](https://github.com/dmarzzz/swarm-dynamics-lab/blob/HEAD/lab/researchers/vishesh/agents/codex-swarm-background.md) · ★3)
- **codex-sync**: Synchronous wrapper around the Codex companion task runtime. Unlike the upstream codex-rescue agent, this always uses foreground mode so the Bash call blocks until Codex finishes and the full result is returned. Use when you need Codex's output as an actual return value for parallel Agent dispatch, for analysis that must be integrated into a report, etc. — not for fire-and-forget background work. ([ThomasEdwardYorke/cc-triad-relay](https://github.com/ThomasEdwardYorke/cc-triad-relay/blob/HEAD/plugins/harness/agents/codex-sync.md) · ★1)

- **codex-talk**: With the Codex runtime enabled (`DEN_CODEX=1`), a first message to Jiao opens a Codex desk run. Bao and Jiao have separate parked desk tickets, `den/00-bao` and `den/00-jiao`, and separate conversations. Desk conversations always belong to the control project. - **coffee-chat**: 커피챗 페르소나 템플릿. 오케스트레이터가 유저 직군에 맞는 유명인 페르소나를 생성하여 호출. ([chenjingdev/chenjing-plugins](https://github.com/chenjingdev/chenjing-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/resume/.claude/agents/coffee-chat.md))
- **coffeeshop.agent**: Coffeeshop agent — behavioral instructions for Claw ([thangchung/agent-engineering-experiment](https://github.com/thangchung/agent-engineering-experiment/blob/HEAD/foundry-agentfx/src/Claw.Agent/mind/.github/agents/coffeeshop.agent.md) · ★27)
- **cofounder**: Business thinking partner that challenges assumptions instead of validating them. Use for business model, go-to-market, pricing, prioritization and hiring calls, AI/automation strategy, and agent-system design framed as a business decision (blast radius, human-in-the-loop, unit economics) - and when the user is juggling several big decisions and needs to separate what is urgent from what feels urgent. For technology selection, build-vs-buy and system architecture, use cto-advisor. ([samhvw8/dotfiles](https://github.com/samhvw8/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/home/.ccp/hub/agents/cofounder.md) · ★15)
- **cofounder**: Use when the founder asks what's going on with their company, what to do next, or wants a status check-in on billing, setup, CRM, or the roadmap. Not for engineering questions about the Cofounder platform itself. ([General-Intelligence-Company/cofounder-plugin](https://github.com/General-Intelligence-Company/cofounder-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/cofounder/agents/cofounder.md) · ★1)
- **cofounder**: Critical-thinking business co-founder that enforces prioritization, challenges assumptions, and becomes smarter over time. Spawned by the /cofounder orchestrator skill. ([rvanbaalen/skills](https://github.com/rvanbaalen/skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/cofounder/agents/cofounder.md))
- **cofounder-agent**: Strategy-layer assistant for a program's cofounder. Helps author and audit the six v1 strategy registries (pitch decks, VPCs, business plans, GTM, financial projections, market analyses) in &lt;program&gt;-strategy. Read-only on business-layer artifacts (outcomes, flows, risks, vocabulary); read-write on strategy-layer staging only. All writes require HITL confirm — the cofounder retains full authority over every artifact. ([inprimex/otaman-plugin](https://github.com/inprimex/otaman-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/cofounder-agent.md) · ★3)
- **cofounder-coach**: **Version:** 1.0 · **Last reviewed:** 2026-05-07 · **Owner:** People Pod ([dahaliztupe-sketch/Kalmeron-Two](https://github.com/dahaliztupe-sketch/Kalmeron-Two/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/cofounder-coach.md) · ★1)
- **cofounder-reviewer**: Use this agent to review a diff or PR against the repo's CoFounder VibeSpecs. Checks for secrets, mock data in prod, scope violations, design-system breaches, missing tests, and deviations from referenced specs. Returns a structured review with confidence-filtered findings — reports only high-priority issues that actually matter. Use proactively after a feature implementation or before opening a PR. ([waymaker-ai/ranavibe](https://github.com/waymaker-ai/ranavibe/blob/HEAD/packages/claude-code-plugin/agents/cofounder-reviewer.md))
- **cofounder-scout**: Spawnable sub-agent variant of the pattern-scout skill. Delegate to this agent when you want a separate, isolated context to find existing patterns in the repo before writing new code — searches for similar functions, components, hooks, models, and routes, then returns ranked matches with extend-vs-create recommendations. Use proactively when the user asks to "add", "build", "create", or "extract" something non-trivial. Distinct from the `cofounder-pattern-scout` skill, which is auto-invoked rather than spawned. ([waymaker-ai/ranavibe](https://github.com/waymaker-ai/ranavibe/blob/HEAD/packages/claude-code-plugin/agents/cofounder-scout.md))
- **cofounder-spec-writer**: Use this agent when the user describes a feature idea but the scope, acceptance criteria, or data sources aren't clear. Asks 3–5 clarifying questions, searches the repo for prior art, and writes a VibeSpec-compliant feature spec to `specs/&lt;slug&gt;.spec.yml`. Use proactively before any non-trivial feature implementation. ([waymaker-ai/ranavibe](https://github.com/waymaker-ai/ranavibe/blob/HEAD/packages/claude-code-plugin/agents/cofounder-spec-writer.md))
- **cog-a11y**: Cognitive accessibility reviewer for ADRs before ratification. ([windyroad/agent-plugins](https://github.com/windyroad/agent-plugins/blob/HEAD/packages/architect/agents/cog-a11y.md) · ★6)
- **cog-architect**: Designs a new cog from a brief. Asks clarifying questions about sensor source, output shape, latency budget, then produces Cargo.toml, cog.toml, and src/main.rs that conform to ADR-091 (cog-sensor-sources) and the cog-development skill. ([cognitum-one/cognitum-claude-plugin](https://github.com/cognitum-one/cognitum-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/cog-dev/agents/cog-architect.md) · ★3)
- **cog-deployer**: Cross-compile + GCS upload + registry bump for a cog. Confirms the rollout by reinstalling on a connected seed via MCP. References the cog-deployment skill for the order of operations. ([cognitum-one/cognitum-claude-plugin](https://github.com/cognitum-one/cognitum-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/cog-dev/agents/cog-deployer.md) · ★3)
- **cog-ops**: Day-2 operations for cogs in the field. Triages logs, checks health, runs OTA, drives rollback. Talks to the seed exclusively over MCP — no SSH. References the cog-operations skill. ([cognitum-one/cognitum-claude-plugin](https://github.com/cognitum-one/cognitum-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/cog-dev/agents/cog-ops.md) · ★3)
- **cog-tester**: Runs the test loop for a cog: cargo check, ARM cross-compile, install on a seed via MCP, smoke-test a few output frames, stop. Reports each step's outcome. ([cognitum-one/cognitum-claude-plugin](https://github.com/cognitum-one/cognitum-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/cog-dev/agents/cog-tester.md) · ★3)
- **cogitator**: Sonnet cruncher-lever warden. Diffs the DSL→cruncher buff extraction before/after a re-authoring pass so cosine-motivated re-phrasing cannot silently drop stackable-buff levers. Use for "check the cruncher levers survived this re-author", "diff coverage for &lt;faction&gt; against the previous state". Prompt must include the touched ability_ids/factions and a before-state reference (committed baseline or a saved coverage snapshot). Returns a single JSON object as final message. ([wn-mitch/40kdc-data](https://github.com/wn-mitch/40kdc-data/blob/HEAD/.omp/agents/cogitator.md) · ★39)
- **cogmo-reviewer**: Read-only conformance reviewer for cogmo. Checks a PR, branch or diff range (`code &lt;target&gt;`) against the repo's rules, idioms, architecture, state-machine, Inngest, data, test and bookkeeping conventions, or a design doc (`design &lt;path&gt;`) against state-machines.md "Design before code". Complements the generic bug-hunting /code-review; use before opening a PR or approving a design. ([iskhakovt/cogmo](https://github.com/iskhakovt/cogmo/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cogmo-reviewer.md))
- **Cognee Memory Agent**: Persistent knowledge graph memory system. Pre-loads context for all agents, stores learnings, enables cross-session intelligence. ([Verridian-ai/life-os-Pulse-banner-generator](https://github.com/Verridian-ai/life-os-Pulse-banner-generator/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cognee-memory-agent.md) · ★1)
- **cognee-recall**: Searches Cognee memory (the permanent knowledge graph, and an indexed repository's code graph) to retrieve relevant context. Can filter by data category (user, project, agent). Memory is auto-recalled on every prompt; use this agent for deeper or targeted searches. ([topoteretes/cognee-integrations](https://github.com/topoteretes/cognee-integrations/blob/HEAD/integrations/claude-code/agents/cognee-recall.md) · ★108)
